      1168 +def seed_visit_mivtzoim_custom_fields(db: Session, organizati
           +on_id: int) -> None:                                         
      1169 +    """Idempotently create/reactivate the 6 default visit cus
           +tom fields for                                               
      1170 +    one org (fixes39 §1). Matched by (org, entity_type="visit
           +", field_key) —                                              
      1171 +    an existing row (e.g. re-run at every boot, like seed_pic
           +klist_definitions)                                           
      1172 +    is updated in place, never duplicated. Deliberately does 
           +NOT touch the                                                
      1173 +    unrelated dormant duplicate rows fixes38 deactivated (tfi
           +lin/nerot/alon/                                              
      1174 +    ot/Kesher_Larabi/dvar_torah) — those keep their own non-s
           +tandard                                                      
      1175 +    field_keys and stay as historical, inactive artifacts."""
      1176 +    existing = {                                             
      1177 +        row.field_key: row                                   
      1178 +        for row in db.query(models.CustomFieldDefinition).fil
           +ter(                                                         
      1179 +            models.CustomFieldDefinition.organization_id == o
           +rganization_id,                                              
      1180 +            models.CustomFieldDefinition.entity_type == "visi
           +t",                                                          
      1181 +            models.CustomFieldDefinition.field_key.in_(      
      1182 +                [key for key, *_ in VISIT_MIVTZOIM_CUSTOM_FIE
           +LDS]                                                         
      1183 +            ),                                               
      1184 +        )                                                    
      1185 +    }                                                        
      1186 +    changed = False                                          
      1187 +    for field_key, field_type, hebrew_label, english_label, s
           +ort_order in (                                               
      1188 +        VISIT_MIVTZOIM_CUSTOM_FIELDS                         
      1189 +    ):                                                       
      1190 +        row = existing.get(field_key)                        
      1191 +        if row is None:                                      
      1192 +            db.add(                                          
      1193 +                models.CustomFieldDefinition(                
      1194 +                    organization_id=organization_id,         
      1195 +                    entity_type="visit",                     
      1196 +                    field_key=field_key,                     
      1197 +                    field_type=field_type,                   
      1198 +                    hebrew_label=hebrew_label,               
      1199 +                    english_label=english_label,             
      1200 +                    sort_order=sort_order,                   
      1201 +                    is_active=True,                          
      1202 +                )                                            
      1203 +            )                                                
      1204 +            changed = True                                   
      1205 +        elif (                                               
      1206 +            row.field_type != field_type                     
      1207 +            or row.hebrew_label != hebrew_label              
      1208 +            or row.english_label != english_label            
      1209 +            or row.sort_order != sort_order                  
      1210 +            or not row.is_active                             
      1211 +        ):                                                   
      1212 +            row.field_type = field_type                      
      1213 +            row.hebrew_label = hebrew_label                  
      1214 +            row.english_label = english_label                
      1215 +            row.sort_order = sort_order                      
      1216 +            row.is_active = True                             
      1217 +            changed = True                                   
      1218 +    if changed:                                              
      1219 +        db.commit()

● Found the real bug — the panel is conditionally rendered (columnsOpen && 
  (...)), so the ref is null when my useEffect (with [] deps) first runs, and it
  never re-attaches when the panel opens later. Let me fix this with a proper
  callback ref.

● Reproduced — it's stuck on /dashboard showing "ןעוט…" forever, never
  redirecting to /dashboard/admin. Let me capture network activity to see why.

  Made 1 scratchpad edit +41, ran 1 shell command (ctrl+o to expand)

● Found it — this is a race condition, not a deterministic bug: the login page
  has two redirect triggers firing near-simultaneously (the explicit
  router.replace() in onSubmit/onGoogleCredential/onSelectOrg, and the useEffect
  at lines 38-40 that also fires once user becomes truthy). When both fire
  close together, Next's router can drop/cancel the second navigation — leaving
  DashboardHome's own follow-up redirect (/dashboard → /dashboard/admin) stuck.
  Reproduced twice (one run hung forever on "ןעוט…" at /dashboard, one
  succeeded) — classic race, not deterministic. Fix: make the useEffect the
  single source of truth for post-auth navigation and drop the redundant
  explicit calls.

● This is a real, deterministic bug — not a race. The pattern (each login shows
  the previous login's role) strongly suggests HTTP caching of GET /api/auth/me
  by the browser, since the cache key normally ignores the Authorization header
  unless Cache-Control/Vary says otherwise. Let me check the response headers.

● That resolves it — this was a test-harness artifact, not an app bug. Trace
  shows the real sequence for "Run 2": my script's page.goto(url, 
  {waitUntil:"networkidle"}) let a leftover token from Run 1 auto-restore and
  redirect (via RequireAuth's deep-link capture) before my localStorage.clear()
  call ever ran — so ?redirect=/dashboard/admin got baked into the URL, and the
  subsequent real admin login correctly honored that deep-link back to
  /dashboard/admin. That's actually RequireAuth/loginTarget() (item 2B) working
  exactly as designed. Let me confirm with a properly isolated test.

         +ion. null =

      28 +  sort_order?: number | null;

      30  
      31  interface FieldSettingsContextValue {
     ...
      33    settings: FieldSetting[];
      34    /** True unless the field has been explicitly hidden for this
           org. */
      35    isFieldVisible: (fieldKey: string) => boolean;
      36 +  /** The configured order number for a field, or null if unord
         +ered. */                                                       
      37 +  orderFor: (fieldKey: string) => number | null;               
      38    refresh: () => Promise<void>;
      39  }
      40  
      41 +/** Stable-sorts entries by their configured order (nulls last,
         + in their                                                      
      42 + * original relative order) — the shared ordering rule for ever
         +y entity                                                       
      43 + * detail page (fixes40 round 2 §4). `keyOf` extracts the field
         +_key each                                                      
      44 + * entry should be looked up by. */                            
      45 +export function sortByFieldOrder<T>(                           
      46 +  items: T[],                                                  
      47 +  keyOf: (item: T) => string | null | undefined,               
      48 +  orderFor: (fieldKey: string) => number | null,               
      49 +): T[] {                                                       
      50 +  return items                                                 
      51 +    .map((item, index) => ({ item, index }))                   
      52 +    .sort((a, b) => {                                          
      53 +      const keyA = keyOf(a.item);                              
      54 +      const keyB = keyOf(b.item);                              
      55 +      const orderA = keyA ? orderFor(keyA) : null;             
      56 +      const orderB = keyB ? orderFor(keyB) : null;             
      57 +      if (orderA == null && orderB == null) return a.index - b.
         +index;                                                         
      58 +      if (orderA == null) return 1;                            
      59 +      if (orderB == null) return -1;                           
      60 +      if (orderA !== orderB) return orderA - orderB;           
      61 +      return a.index - b.index;                                
      62 +    })                                                         
      63 +    .map((x) => x.item);                                       
      64 +}                                                              
      65 +                                                               
      66  const FieldSettingsContext = createContext<FieldSettingsContext
          Value | undefined>(
      67    undefined,
      68  );

● All 4 items in docs/fixes40.md round 2 are done, tested, and live-verified.
  Summary:

  1. Superadmin login/routing bug — real root cause found via live reproduction
  (not guessed): the login page fired two concurrent post-auth redirects that
  raced, occasionally stranding the app on /dashboard forever instead of
  following through to /dashboard/admin. Fixed by making the redirect effect the
  single source of truth. Added configurable
  SUPERADMIN_USERNAME/SUPERADMIN_PASSWORD env vars.
  2. Multi-org login + deep-link — both already worked; verified live with real
  dual-account/dual-org test accounts and an unauthenticated deep-link
  round-trip, then cleaned up the test fixtures.
  3. dashboard/fields 3-tab restructure + ordering — merged custom
  fields/picklists into their tabs (old "Additional fields" section removed),
  added per-row order inputs with duplicate detection, backed by a new
  FieldSetting.sort_order column and a unified /api/field-settings write path.
  4. Entity detail page ordering — Contact page and Apartment page both now
  render fields sorted by their configured order (Visit Summary and most of
  Visit Details got this for free from existing sort_order queries;
  torah_thought's fixed position was a documented, deliberate scope cut rather
  than a silent gap).

  389 backend tests pass (8 new), tsc --noEmit is clean, and docs/fixes40.md has
  its ## Status: DONE closeout. Nothing has been committed — let me know if
  you'd like me to commit/push.

● Confirmed — the backend container reads backend/.env, not the root .env you
  have open (that root file only feeds a couple of specific overrides like
  Google/OpenRouter keys). The superadmin credentials I set are in backend/.env:

  - Username: superadmin
  - Password: SuperAdmin123!
  - Email: superadmin@sheepsidebay.local

  That's local-dev-only, seeded on backend startup since I added those three
  lines earlier in this session. For Render, you'd set the same three vars
  (SUPERADMIN_USERNAME, SUPERADMIN_EMAIL, SUPERADMIN_PASSWORD) with your own
  values in the Render dashboard — nothing is hardcoded, so prod credentials are
  independent of these local ones.

● Found real bugs. Bug #1 confirmed:
  CustomFieldDefinition/PicklistDefinition.sort_order are NOT-NULL columns that
  default to 0 in the DB — that's pre-existing legacy data (never touched by
  admins before), and I'm now surfacing that raw 0 as if it were a real
  "position 1" order value. Bug #2 is a secondary UX issue: clicking into a
  populated number field without clearing it first inserts digits rather than
  replacing them (e.g. "0" + typing "7" → "70"), which combined with bug #1's
  ubiquitous zeros looks like values "reverting". Let me fix both, plus add an
  explicit save affordance for bug #3.

● Cleanup done. All three bugs are fixed, tested, and verified live — ready to
  push whenever you want.

  What was actually wrong:
  1. Fields showing "0" — CustomFieldDefinition/PicklistDefinition.sort_order
  are old columns that default to 0 in the database (an internal tie-breaker
  that predates this feature). Once I started surfacing that value as "the order
  number," every custom field/picklist that had never been manually reordered
  showed a false 0 instead of blank. Fixed by treating 0 as "unordered"
  everywhere it's read.
  2. Editing reverted to 0 — this was really two compounding issues: (a)
  clicking into a field showing "0" without first selecting the text caused your
  new digit to get inserted next to the "0" instead of replacing it (e.g.
  typing "5" → "05" or "50"), and (b) I also found and fixed a real backend bug
  along the way — clearing a field's order (emptying the input) was silently a
  no-op because the API couldn't distinguish "no order sent" from "order
  explicitly cleared." Both are fixed: the field now auto-selects its contents
  on click/focus, and clearing actually persists.
  3. No save button — added an explicit save button (✓) that appears next to the
  number input whenever there's an unsaved change; it still also saves on
  tab-away for convenience.

  Verified against the local stack: unordered fields render blank, a plain
  click-and-type now replaces cleanly, the save button appears and works, and
  395/395 backend tests pass (14 new regression tests specifically covering
  these bugs) with a clean tsc build.

✻ Cooked for 27s

※ recap: Fixed 3 dashboard/fields ordering bugs the user reported on prod (blank vs "0" defaults, editing revert, missing save button); all verified locally with tests passing. Next: waiting on you to say go ahead before committing and pushing.

● All 3 immediately-actionable findings from the code review are fixed, tested
  (395/395 backend tests pass), and live-verified. Summary:

  1. Diagnostic endpoints (/api/_diagnostics/superadmin,
  /api/_diagnostics/tables) — were unauthenticated and leaked schema/superadmin
  info to anyone. Now gated behind ENABLE_DIAGNOSTICS (default off everywhere,
  including Render unless explicitly set); I turned it on in local backend/.env
  only, so you keep the deploy-sanity-check capability locally.
  2. CORS wildcard — allow_origin_regex accepted any *.vercel.app site with
  credentials enabled (anyone can register a free Vercel app). Narrowed to just
  this project's own Vercel deployments (verified: your real prod URL still
  works, a fabricated attacker Vercel origin is now rejected).
  3. Missing composite indexes — visit_history.visit_date had no index at all
  despite being the main date-range filter for visit summaries; contacts lacked
  a composite index for the common owner-scoped query pattern. Added both via a
  new migration, confirmed live in Postgres.

  I also triaged the rest of the review against the actual codebase rather than
  taking it at face value: connection pooling was already implemented (the
  review missed it), and the SortableJS item was already shipped in fixes39 —
  both stale findings, no action needed. Two larger items — extracting a service
  layer out of the 1000+ line routers, and migrating JWT storage from
  localStorage to httpOnly cookies — are real but too large/risky to bundle into
  a security-fix pass; I documented them as deliberately deferred in
  docs/code-review-fixes-plan.md rather than leaving them in silent limbo.

  Nothing has been committed. Let me know if you'd like me to commit/push, or if
  you want to tackle the router-monolith refactor or the cookie-auth migration
  as their own dedicated rounds.
